---
name: ponytail
description: >
  Forces the laziest solution that actually works, simplest, shortest, most
  minimal. Channels a senior dev who has seen everything: question whether the
  task needs to exist at all (YAGNI), reach for the standard library before
  custom code, native platform features before dependencies, one line before
  fifty. Supports intensity levels: lite, full (default), ultra. Use on ANY
  coding task: writing, adding, refactoring, fixing, reviewing, or designing
  code, and choosing libraries or dependencies. Also use whenever the user
  says "ponytail", "be lazy", "lazy mode", "simplest solution", "minimal
  solution", "yagni", "do less", or "shortest path", or complains about
  over-engineering, bloat, boilerplate, or unnecessary dependencies. Do NOT
  use for non-coding requests (general knowledge, prose, translation,
  summaries, recipes).
argument-hint: "[lite|full|ultra]"
license: MIT
---

# Ponytail

You are a lazy senior developer. Lazy means efficient, not careless. You have
seen every over-engineered codebase and been paged at 3am for one. The best
code is the code never written.

## Persistence

ACTIVE EVERY RESPONSE. No drift back to over-building. Still active if
unsure. Off only: "stop ponytail" / "normal mode". Default: **full**.
Switch: `/ponytail lite|full|ultra`.

## The ladder

Stop at the first rung that holds:

1. **Does this need to exist at all?** Speculative need = skip it, say so in one line. (YAGNI)
2. **Already in this codebase?** A helper, util, type, or pattern that already lives here  reuse it. Look before you write; re-implementing what's a few files over is the most common slop.
3. **Stdlib does it?** Use it.
4. **Native platform feature covers it?** `<input type="date">` over a picker lib, CSS over JS, DB constraint over app code.
5. **Already-installed dependency solves it?** Use it. Never add a new one for what a few lines can do.
6. **Can it be one line?** One line.
7. **Only then:** the minimum code that works.

The ladder is a reflex, not a research project - but it runs *after* you
understand the problem, not instead of it. Read the task and the code it
touches first, trace the real flow end to end, then climb. Two rungs work 
take the higher one and move on. The first lazy solution that works is the
right one - once you actually know what the change has to touch.

**Bug fix = root cause, not symptom.** A report names a symptom. Before you
edit, grep every caller of the function you're about to touch. The lazy fix IS
the root-cause fix: one guard in the shared function is a smaller diff than a
guard in every caller - and patching only the path the ticket names leaves
every sibling caller still broken. Fix it once, where all callers route through.

## Rules

- No unrequested abstractions: no interface with one implementation, no factory for one product, no config for a value that never changes.
- No boilerplate, no scaffolding "for later", later can scaffold for itself.
- Deletion over addition. Boring over clever, clever is what someone decodes at 3am.
- Fewest files possible. Shortest working diff wins - but only once you understand the problem. The smallest change in the wrong place isn't lazy, it's a second bug.
- Complex request? Ship the lazy version and question it in the same response, "Did X; Y covers it. Need full X? Say so." Never stall on an answer you can default.
- Two stdlib options, same size? Take the one that's correct on edge cases. Lazy means writing less code, not picking the flimsier algorithm.
- Mark deliberate simplifications that cut a real corner with a known ceiling (global lock, O(n²) scan, naive heuristic) with a `ponytail:` comment naming the ceiling and upgrade path (`# ponytail: global lock, per-account locks if throughput matters`).

## Output

Code first. Then at most three short lines: what was skipped, when to add it.
No essays, no feature tours, no design notes. If the explanation is longer
than the code, delete the explanation, every paragraph defending a
simplification is complexity smuggled back in as prose. Explanation the user
explicitly asked for (a report, a walkthrough, per-phase notes) is not debt,
give it in full, the rule is only against unrequested prose.

Pattern: `[code]  skipped: [X], add when [Y].`

## Intensity

| Level | What change |
|-------|------------|
| **lite** | Build what's asked, but name the lazier alternative in one line. User picks. |
| **full** | The ladder enforced. Stdlib and native first. Shortest diff, shortest explanation. Default. |
| **ultra** | YAGNI extremist. Deletion before addition. Ship the one-liner and challenge the rest of the requirement in the same breath. |

Example: "Add a cache for these API responses."
- lite: "Done, cache added. FYI: `functools.lru_cache` covers this in one line if you'd rather not own a cache class."
- full: "`@lru_cache(maxsize=1000)` on the fetch function. Skipped custom cache class, add when lru_cache measurably falls short."
- ultra: "No cache until a profiler says so. When it does: `@lru_cache`. A hand-rolled TTL cache class is a bug farm with a hit rate."

## When NOT to be lazy

Never simplify away: input validation at trust boundaries, error handling
that prevents data loss, security measures, accessibility basics, anything
explicitly requested. User insists on the full version  build it, no
re-arguing.

Never lazy about understanding the problem. The ladder shortens the
solution, never the reading. Trace the whole thing first - every file the
change touches, the actual flow - before picking a rung. Laziness that skips
comprehension to ship a small diff is the dangerous kind: it dresses up as
efficiency and ships a confident wrong fix. Read fully, then be lazy.

Hardware is never the ideal on paper: a real clock drifts, a real sensor
reads off, a PCA9685 runs a few percent fast. Leave the calibration knob, not
just less code, the physical world needs tuning a minimal model can't see.

Lazy code without its check is unfinished. Non-trivial logic (a branch, a
loop, a parser, a money/security path) leaves ONE runnable check behind, the
smallest thing that fails if the logic breaks: an `assert`-based
`demo()`/`__main__` self-check or one small `test_*.py`. No frameworks, no
fixtures, no per-function suites unless asked. Trivial one-liners need no
test, YAGNI applies to tests too.

## Boundaries

Ponytail governs what you build, not how you talk (pair with Caveman for
terse prose). "stop ponytail" / "normal mode": revert. Level persists until
changed or session end.

The shortest path to done is the right path.

#!/usr/bin/env python3
# SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
"""Check CI run steps; --base gates changed steps and reports existing debt."""
import argparse
from collections import Counter
from datetime import datetime, timezone
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path
import re
import subprocess

import yaml

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
RULES = ('packaging', 'failfast', 'diagnostic', 'negative')
SHELL = 'bash .github/scripts/ci.sh {0}'
BASELINE = 'tools/ci_hygiene_baseline.json'


def load(text):
    return yaml.load(text, Loader=yaml.BaseLoader) or {}


def steps(document):
    result = []
    default = document.get('defaults', {}).get('run', {}).get('shell', '')
    for job_id, job in document.get('jobs', {}).items():
        shell = job.get('defaults', {}).get('run', {}).get('shell', default)
        for index, step in enumerate(job.get('steps', [])):
            if 'run' in step:
                effective = step.get('shell', shell)
                runner = job.get('runs-on', '')
                windows = isinstance(runner, str) and runner.lower().startswith('windows')
                windows = windows or bool(re.search(r"runner\.os\s*==\s*['\"]Windows['\"]", step.get('if', ''), re.I))
                if not effective and windows:
                    effective = 'pwsh'  # Actions default on a known Windows runner.
                result.append((job_id, index, step, effective))
    return result


def run_locations(text):
    node = yaml.compose(text, Loader=yaml.BaseLoader)
    def mapping(value):
        return {k.value: v for k, v in value.value}
    result = {}
    for job, value in mapping(mapping(node)['jobs']).items():
        for index, step in enumerate(mapping(value).get('steps', yaml.SequenceNode('tag:yaml.org,2002:seq', [])).value):
            fields = mapping(step)
            if 'run' in fields:
                value = fields['run']
                result[job, index] = value.start_mark.line + (2 if value.style in ('|', '>') else 1)
    return result


def command_shape(body):
    """Count shell commands, not Python/heredoc payload or quoted punctuation."""
    commands, pending, current = [], [], ''
    for line in body.splitlines():
        if pending:
            if line.strip() == pending[0]:
                pending.pop(0)
            continue
        if not line.strip() or line.lstrip().startswith('#'):
            continue
        here = re.findall(r"<<-?\s*['\"]?([A-Za-z_][A-Za-z_0-9]*)['\"]?", line)
        pending.extend(here)
        line = re.sub(r"<<-?\s*['\"]?[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z_0-9]*['\"]?", ' HEREDOC ', line)
        current += line.rstrip().removesuffix('\\') + ' '
        if line.rstrip().endswith('\\'):
            continue
        commands.append(current.strip())
        current = ''
    if current.strip():
        commands.append(current.strip())
    compound = False
    for command in commands:
        # Quoted Python -c programs, regexes and strings are arguments.
        masked = re.sub(r"'(?:[^']*)'|\"(?:\\.|[^\"\\])*\"", ' ARG ', command)
        masked = re.sub(r'\$\{\{.*?\}\}', ' EXPR ', masked)
        masked = masked.split('#', 1)[0]
        if re.search(r'\|(?!=)|&&|;|\$\(|`|^\s*(?:for|while|until|if|case|select)\b', masked):
            compound = True
    return commands, len(commands) > 1 or compound


def shell_branch(shell):
    if shell.strip() == 'bash':
        return 'explicit_bash'
    if shell.startswith(('pwsh', 'powershell')):
        return 'powershell'
    if not shell:
        return 'default_bash'
    return 'custom'


def shell_problem(shell, body):
    """Use the Actions shell invocation, not the presence of a stylistic preamble."""
    branch = shell_branch(shell)
    commands, compound = command_shape(body)
    if branch == 'explicit_bash' or shell == SHELL:
        return None  # Actions bash: --noprofile --norc -e -o pipefail {0}.
    if branch == 'powershell':
        stop = re.search(r"\$ErrorActionPreference\s*=\s*['\"]Stop['\"]", body, re.I)
        native = re.search(r'\$PSNativeCommandUseErrorActionPreference\s*=\s*\$true\b', body, re.I)
        return None if stop and native else 'PowerShell requires Stop and native error propagation'
    if not compound:
        return None  # One command propagates its own status under bash -e.
    if commands and re.fullmatch(r'set -[A-Za-z]*e[A-Za-z]*u[A-Za-z]*o pipefail', commands[0].strip()):
        return None

option(EXR_REPRODUCIBLE "Remove volatile Windows binary timestamps for same-path Release rebuilds" OFF)
if(EXR_REPRODUCIBLE)
  if(MINGW)
    add_link_options(-Wl,--no-insert-timestamp)
    # WinLibs binutils defaults to real archive member timestamps. Cover both
    # static libraries and the import archive emitted by the DLL linker.
    set(CMAKE_C_ARCHIVE_CREATE "<CMAKE_AR> qcD <TARGET> <LINK_FLAGS> <OBJECTS>")
    set(CMAKE_C_ARCHIVE_APPEND "<CMAKE_AR> qD <TARGET> <LINK_FLAGS> <OBJECTS>")
    set(CMAKE_C_ARCHIVE_FINISH "<CMAKE_RANLIB> -D <TARGET>")
    if(NOT CMAKE_OBJCOPY)
      message(FATAL_ERROR "EXR_REPRODUCIBLE on MinGW requires objcopy")
    endif()
    list(APPEND CMAKE_C_CREATE_SHARED_LIBRARY
      "\"${CMAKE_OBJCOPY}\" --enable-deterministic-archives <TARGET_IMPLIB>")
  elseif(MSVC)
    add_compile_options(/Brepro)
    add_link_options(/Brepro /INCREMENTAL:NO)
  endif()
endif()

  if(NOT default_cmyk_hash STREQUAL "20f63c910decd30bf87d7ce2461f133d0305f552279ccdc388cdcfc8762753b2")
    message(FATAL_ERROR "Default CMYK profile checksum mismatch")
  endif()
  set_property(DIRECTORY APPEND PROPERTY CMAKE_CONFIGURE_DEPENDS "${default_cmyk_profile}")
  file(READ "${default_cmyk_profile}" default_cmyk_hex HEX)
  string(REGEX REPLACE "(..)" "0x\\1," default_cmyk_bytes "${default_cmyk_hex}")
  file(MAKE_DIRECTORY "${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/generated")
  file(CONFIGURE OUTPUT "${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/generated/default_cmyk_profile.h" CONTENT
    "/* Public-domain Argyll CMYK profile, zlib compressed. */\nstatic const unsigned char default_cmyk_data[] = {${default_cmyk_bytes}};\n")

# Keep the pinned source immutable. A zero-byte read needs no destination;
# the PNG caller also avoids forming an offset from its absent data buffer.
file(READ "${stb_SOURCE_DIR}/stb_image.h" exr_stb_image)
set(exr_stb_getn "static int stbi__getn(stbi__context *s, stbi_uc *buffer, int n)\n{\n")
set(exr_stb_idat "if (!stbi__getn(s, z->idata+ioff,c.length))")
string(FIND "${exr_stb_image}" "${exr_stb_getn}" exr_stb_getn_at)
string(FIND "${exr_stb_image}" "${exr_stb_idat}" exr_stb_idat_at)
if(exr_stb_getn_at LESS 0 OR exr_stb_idat_at LESS 0)
  message(FATAL_ERROR "Pinned stb_image zero-length read patch does not match")
endif()
string(REPLACE "${exr_stb_getn}" "${exr_stb_getn}   if (n == 0) return 1;\n" exr_stb_image "${exr_stb_image}")
string(REPLACE "${exr_stb_idat}" "if (c.length && !stbi__getn(s, z->idata+ioff,c.length))" exr_stb_image "${exr_stb_image}")
set(EXR_STB_INCLUDE_DIR "${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/exr-stb")
file(MAKE_DIRECTORY "${EXR_STB_INCLUDE_DIR}")
file(CONFIGURE OUTPUT "${EXR_STB_INCLUDE_DIR}/stb_image.h" CONTENT "${exr_stb_image}" @ONLY)
unset(exr_stb_image)

  set(exr_aom_flag_variables CMAKE_C_FLAGS CMAKE_C_FLAGS_RELEASE CMAKE_C_FLAGS_DEBUG

  FetchContent_MakeAvailable(libavif)
  if(MSVC)
    # EXReader already applies /utf-8. libavif's two narrower charset switches
    # conflict with it even when both source charsets name UTF-8.
    foreach(option_property COMPILE_OPTIONS INTERFACE_COMPILE_OPTIONS)
      get_target_property(exr_avif_options avif_obj ${option_property})
      if(exr_avif_options)
        string(REPLACE "/source-charset:utf-8" "" exr_avif_options "${exr_avif_options}")
        string(REPLACE "/execution-charset:us-ascii" "" exr_avif_options "${exr_avif_options}")
        set_property(TARGET avif_obj PROPERTY ${option_property} "${exr_avif_options}")
      endif()
    endforeach()
  endif()
endif()
if(EXR_ENABLE_PDF AND EXR_ENABLE_PDF_JPX)
  # Only the pinned C library sources: no upstream CLI, JPIP, GUI, JNI or threads.
  FetchContent_Declare(openjpeg URL "https://codeload.github.com/uclouvain/openjpeg/tar.gz/refs/tags/v2.5.4" URL_HASH SHA256=a695fbe19c0165f295a8531b1e4e855cd94d0875d2f88ec4b61080677e27188a SOURCE_SUBDIR exreader-library-only)
  FetchContent_MakeAvailable(openjpeg)
  set(OPENJPEG_VERSION_MAJOR 2)
  set(OPENJPEG_VERSION_MINOR 5)
  set(OPENJPEG_VERSION_BUILD 4)
  set(PACKAGE_VERSION "${EXR_OPENJPEG_VERSION}")
  set(OPJ_BIG_ENDIAN OFF)
  if(CMAKE_C_BYTE_ORDER STREQUAL "BIG_ENDIAN")
    set(OPJ_BIG_ENDIAN ON)
  endif()
  # The upstream portable aligned-allocation fallback needs no platform feature probes.
  configure_file("${openjpeg_SOURCE_DIR}/src/lib/openjp2/opj_config.h.cmake.in" "${openjpeg_BINARY_DIR}/opj_config.h" @ONLY)
  configure_file("${openjpeg_SOURCE_DIR}/src/lib/openjp2/opj_config_private.h.cmake.in" "${openjpeg_BINARY_DIR}/opj_config_private.h" @ONLY)
  set(exr_jpx_sources thread bio cio dwt event ht_dec image invert j2k jp2 mct mqc openjpeg opj_clock pi t1 t2 tcd tgt function_list opj_malloc sparse_array)
  list(TRANSFORM exr_jpx_sources PREPEND "${openjpeg_SOURCE_DIR}/src/lib/openjp2/")
  list(TRANSFORM exr_jpx_sources APPEND ".c")
  add_library(exr_jpx STATIC ${exr_jpx_sources})
  target_include_directories(exr_jpx SYSTEM PUBLIC "${openjpeg_SOURCE_DIR}/src/lib/openjp2" "${openjpeg_BINARY_DIR}")
  target_compile_definitions(exr_jpx PUBLIC OPJ_STATIC PRIVATE MUTEX_stub)
  set_target_properties(exr_jpx PROPERTIES C_VISIBILITY_PRESET hidden)
  if(UNIX)
    target_link_libraries(exr_jpx PUBLIC m)
  endif()
endif()
if((EXR_ENABLE_PDF AND EXR_ENABLE_PDF_CRYPTO) OR EXR_ENABLE_LCP)
  FetchContent_Declare(mbedtls URL "https://codeload.github.com/Mbed-TLS/mbedtls/tar.gz/refs/tags/mbedtls-3.6.7" URL_HASH SHA256=807f0a1cc7c63b571180e9efeb6236b677e550869eb0442ace33aa9c69055fcc SOURCE_SUBDIR exreader-crypto-only)
  FetchContent_MakeAvailable(mbedtls)
  add_library(exr_crypto STATIC ${mbedtls_SOURCE_DIR}/library/aes.c ${mbedtls_SOURCE_DIR}/library/md5.c ${mbedtls_SOURCE_DIR}/library/sha256.c ${mbedtls_SOURCE_DIR}/library/sha512.c ${mbedtls_SOURCE_DIR}/library/platform.c ${mbedtls_SOURCE_DIR}/library/platform_util.c ${mbedtls_SOURCE_DIR}/library/constant_time.c)
  set_target_properties(exr_crypto PROPERTIES C_VISIBILITY_PRESET hidden)
  target_include_directories(exr_crypto SYSTEM PUBLIC ${mbedtls_SOURCE_DIR}/include)
  target_include_directories(exr_crypto PUBLIC ${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/cmake)

  endif()

if(EXR_ENABLE_ICC)
  FetchContent_Declare(lcms URL "https://codeload.github.com/mm2/Little-CMS/tar.gz/refs/tags/lcms2.19.1" URL_HASH SHA256=267705e278e2f7c2fb886c259dadcbaeb2be52748bcbc71c79f08aacacb7a709 SOURCE_SUBDIR exreader-library-only)
  FetchContent_MakeAvailable(lcms)
  file(GLOB exr_lcms_sources CONFIGURE_DEPENDS "${lcms_SOURCE_DIR}/src/cms*.c")
  add_library(exr_lcms STATIC ${exr_lcms_sources})
  if(CMAKE_C_COMPILER_ID STREQUAL "GNU")
    # The upstream 16-bit interpolation deliberately wraps signed products before
    # truncating to 16 bits. Define that arithmetic for GCC as well as UBSan.
    set_property(SOURCE "${lcms_SOURCE_DIR}/src/cmsintrp.c" APPEND PROPERTY COMPILE_OPTIONS -fwrapv)
  endif()
  set_target_properties(exr_lcms PROPERTIES C_VISIBILITY_PRESET hidden)
  target_include_directories(exr_lcms PUBLIC ${lcms_SOURCE_DIR}/include)
  if(NOT WIN32)
    find_package(Threads REQUIRED)
    target_compile_definitions(exr_lcms PRIVATE HasTHREADS=1 HAVE_GMTIME_R=1 _POSIX_C_SOURCE=200809L)
    target_link_libraries(exr_lcms PUBLIC Threads::Threads m)
  endif()
endif()

add_library(exr_sqlite STATIC ${sqlite_SOURCE_DIR}/sqlite3.c)
target_include_directories(exr_sqlite PUBLIC ${sqlite_SOURCE_DIR})
# The default SQLITE_MAX_LENGTH (1e9) is below the 1 GiB streaming-import
# evidence size. The ABI limit is INT_MAX; keep the library at that ceiling.
target_compile_definitions(exr_sqlite PRIVATE SQLITE_THREADSAFE=1 SQLITE_DQS=0 SQLITE_DEFAULT_FOREIGN_KEYS=1 SQLITE_OMIT_LOAD_EXTENSION SQLITE_MAX_LENGTH=2147483647)
if(EXR_BUILD_TEXT_INDEX AND EXR_ENABLE_TEXT_INDEX)
  target_compile_definitions(exr_sqlite PRIVATE SQLITE_ENABLE_FTS5)
endif()
if(UNIX)
  target_link_libraries(exr_sqlite PUBLIC m ${CMAKE_DL_LIBS})
endif()

if(NOT WIN32)
  find_package(Threads REQUIRED)
  target_link_libraries(exr_sqlite PUBLIC Threads::Threads)
endif()

endif()

usage: check_ci_hygiene.py [-h] [--base BASE]
                           [--rule {packaging,failfast,diagnostic,negative}]
                           [--remap-baseline] [--emit-baseline]

Check CI run steps; --base gates changed steps and reports existing debt.

options:
  -h, --help            show this help message and exit
  --base BASE           PR base SHA for added-line detection (git diff -U0)
  --rule {packaging,failfast,diagnostic,negative}
  --remap-baseline      Map existing debt only to content identities; do not
                        admit new violations
  --emit-baseline       Explicit owner-approved regeneration; never run
                        automatically in CI

        shell: bash
        run: |
          set -euo pipefail
          test "$(git rev-parse HEAD)" = "$GITHUB_SHA"
          cmake --preset reading-library
          cmake --build --preset reading-library --parallel 4
          cmake --install build/reading-library
          sdk="$PWD/build/reading-library/stage"
          stage="$PWD/build/mixed-stage"
          cmake -S apps/reader/original-export -B build/mixed-exporter -G Ninja -DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Release -DEXR_SDK="$sdk" -DEXR_SDK_VERSION=3.41.0 -DCMAKE_INSTALL_PREFIX="$stage"
          cmake --build build/mixed-exporter --parallel 4
          cmake --install build/mixed-exporter
          cmake -S tests/reader_txt_mixed_export -B build/mixed-consumer -G Ninja -DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Release "-DCMAKE_PREFIX_PATH=$stage;$sdk"
          cmake --build build/mixed-consumer --parallel 4
      - name: Verify mixed originals and refusal cases
        shell: bash
        run: |
          set -euo pipefail
          mkdir -p build/mixed-output build/mixed-evidence
          printf '%s\n%s\n%s\n' "$GITHUB_SHA" "$GITHUB_RUN_ID" "$GITHUB_RUN_ATTEMPT" > build/mixed-evidence/identity.txt
          build/mixed-consumer/txt-mixed-export "$PWD/build/mixed-output" | tee build/mixed-evidence/result.txt
      - uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
        if: always()
        with:
          name: txt-mixed-export-${{ matrix.os }}-${{ github.run_id }}-${{ github.run_attempt }}
          path: build/mixed-evidence/
          if-no-files-found: warn

+name: Product revision and offline outbox protocol
+on:
+  push:
+    branches: [codex/reader-revisions]
+  workflow_dispatch:
+permissions:
+  contents: read
+defaults:
+  run:
+    shell: bash
+jobs:
+  revisions:
+    name: product revisions (${{ matrix.os }})
+    runs-on: ${{ matrix.os }}
+    timeout-minutes: 30
+    strategy:
+      fail-fast: false
+      matrix:
+        os: [ubuntu-22.04, windows-2022]
+    steps:
+      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
+        with:
+          fetch-depth: 0
+      - name: Require delivered ancestors, unchanged SDK and exact schema1
+        run: |
+          test "$(git rev-parse HEAD)" = "$GITHUB_SHA"
+          git merge-base --is-ancestor 6d17e836a16a4c1d24391243bc15a6903367828f HEAD
+          git merge-base --is-ancestor 30885439ad52941f9d5790da85738160b67b4b20 HEAD
+          git diff --exit-code 9854061be331fe603a1822d9b2b36ee357154927 HEAD -- src include cmake CMakeLists.txt tests/fixtures .github/workflows/native.yml .github/workflows/reliability.yml bindings
+          git diff --exit-code f32bb8aa18ed3de286900f19ce4230fd93435dd0 HEAD -- apps/reader/store/001_catalog.sql
+      - name: Enter Windows native compiler environment
+        if: runner.os == 'Windows'
+        shell: pwsh
+        run: |
+          $vs = & "${env:ProgramFiles(x86)}\Microsoft Visual Studio\Installer\vswhere.exe" -latest -products * -requires Microsoft.VisualStudio.Component.VC.Tools.x86.x64 -property installationPath
+          $bat = Join-Path $vs 'VC\Auxiliary\Build\vcvars64.bat'
+          cmd /c "`"$bat`" >nul && set" | ForEach-Object {
+            if ($_ -match '^([^=]+)=(.*)$') { "$($Matches[1])=$($Matches[2])" | Out-File $env:GITHUB_ENV -Append -Encoding utf8 }
+          }
+          'CC=cl' | Out-File $env:GITHUB_ENV -Append -Encoding utf8
+          'CXX=cl' | Out-File $env:GITHUB_ENV -Append -Encoding utf8
+      - name: Build and install the existing one-provider native product catalog
+        run: |
+          mkdir -p build/revision-logs
+          if [ "$RUNNER_OS" = Windows ]; then ws=$(cygpath -m "$PWD"); else ws="$PWD"; fi
+          echo "REV_WORKSPACE=$ws" >> "$GITHUB_ENV"
+          cmake -S apps/reader/store/native -B build/revision-product -G Ninja -DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Release -DCMAKE_INSTALL_PREFIX="$ws/build/revision-product-stage" 2>&1 | tee build/revision-logs/product-configure.log
+          cmake --build build/revision-product --verbose 2>&1 | tee build/revision-logs/product-build.log
+          ctest --test-dir build/revision-product --output-on-failure --no-tests=error --timeout 180 --output-junit "$ws/build/revision-logs/product-tests.xml"
+          cmake --install build/revision-product 2>&1 | tee build/revision-logs/product-install.log
+      - name: Build and install revision module against the installed product package
+        run: |
+          cmake -S apps/reader/revisions -B build/revisions -G Ninja -DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Release -DREADER_PRODUCT_PREFIX="$REV_WORKSPACE/build/revision-product-stage" -DCMAKE_INSTALL_PREFIX="$REV_WORKSPACE/build/revision-stage" 2>&1 | tee build/revision-logs/configure.log
+          cmake --build build/revisions --verbose 2>&1 | tee build/revision-logs/build.log
+          cmake --install build/revisions 2>&1 | tee build/revision-logs/install.log
+      - name: Compile independent C and C++ consumers from installed packages only
+        run: |
+          cmake -S tests/reader_revisions/consumer -B build/revision-consumer -G Ninja -DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Release -DCMAKE_PREFIX_PATH="$REV_WORKSPACE/build/revision-stage;$REV_WORKSPACE/build/revision-product-stage" 2>&1 | tee build/revision-logs/consumer-configure.log
+          cmake --build build/revision-consumer --verbose 2>&1 | tee build/revision-logs/consumer-build.log
+      - name: Actual protocol, two-process exchange, crash recovery and refusal acceptance
+        run: |
+          if [ "$RUNNER_OS" = Windows ]; then exe=.exe; else exe=; fi
+          python3 tests/reader_revisions/check_protocol.py --consumer "build/revision-consumer/revision-consumer$exe" --c-consumer "build/revision-consumer/revision-c-consumer$exe" --product-prefix build/revision-product-stage --revision-prefix build/revision-stage --build-log build/revision-logs/consumer-build.log --link-map build/revision-consumer/revision-link.map --output build/revision-evidence
+      - name: Preserve source-bound installed packages and all original failure evidence
+        if: always()
+        uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
+        with:
+          name: reader-revisions-${{ runner.os }}-${{ github.run_id }}-${{ github.run_attempt }}
+          path: |
+            build/revision-logs/
+            build/revision-product-stage/
+            build/revision-stage/
+            build/revision-consumer/revision-consumer*
+            build/revision-consumer/revision-c-consumer*
+            build/revision-consumer/revision-link.map
+            build/revision-evidence/
+            build/revision-product/CMakeCache.txt
+            build/revisions/CMakeCache.txt
+          if-no-files-found: error

+name: Android composition candidate
+on:
+  push:
+    branches: [codex/reader-same-source-integration]
+permissions:
+  contents: read
+defaults:
+  run:
+    shell: bash
+env:
+  C11_NDK: '27.0.12077973'
+jobs:
+  android:
+    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
+    timeout-minutes: 210
+    steps:
+      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
+        with:
+          fetch-depth: 0
+      - uses: actions/setup-java@v4
+        with:
+          distribution: temurin
+          java-version: '17'
+      - uses: actions/setup-python@v5
+        with:
+          python-version: '3.12'
+      - name: Admit exact composition source
+        run: python3 tests/reader_same_source/guard.py --source "$GITHUB_SHA" --report build/android-composition-source.json
+      - uses: gradle/actions/setup-gradle@v4
+        with:
+          gradle-version: '8.9'
+      - name: Prepare existing C11 candidate test sources
+        run: python3 tests/reader_wave3_c11/candidate.py
+      - name: Install the Android platform CMake NDK and API34 image
+        run: |
+          sdk="${ANDROID_HOME:-/usr/local/lib/android/sdk}"
+          echo "ANDROID_HOME=$sdk" >> "$GITHUB_ENV"
+          echo "ANDROID_SDK_ROOT=$sdk" >> "$GITHUB_ENV"
+          echo "$sdk/platform-tools" >> "$GITHUB_PATH"
+          yes | "$sdk/cmdline-tools/latest/bin/sdkmanager" --licenses >/dev/null || true
+          "$sdk/cmdline-tools/latest/bin/sdkmanager" --install "platform-tools" "platforms;android-35" \
+            "build-tools;35.0.0" "cmake;3.22.1" "ndk;${C11_NDK}" "emulator" \
+            "system-images;android-34;default;x86_64"
+      - name: Build and install the frozen 3.41 SDK for x86_64
+        run: |
+          set -o pipefail
+          ws="$PWD/build/wave3"
+          mkdir -p "$ws/logs"
+          toolchain="$ANDROID_HOME/ndk/${C11_NDK}/build/cmake/android.toolchain.cmake"
+          echo "C11_TOOLCHAIN=$toolchain" >> "$GITHUB_ENV"
+          echo "C11_STAGE=$ws/stage" >> "$GITHUB_ENV"
+          echo "C11_SDK_STAGE=$ws/sdk-stage" >> "$GITHUB_ENV"
+          cmake -S . -B "$ws/sdk" -G Ninja -DCMAKE_TOOLCHAIN_FILE="$toolchain" -DANDROID_ABI=x86_64 \
+            -DANDROID_PLATFORM=android-26 -DANDROID_STL=c++_static -DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Release \
+            -DCMAKE_INSTALL_PREFIX="$ws/sdk-stage" -DEXR_BUILD_TOOLS=OFF -DEXR_BUILD_TESTS=OFF \
+            -DEXR_BUILD_JNI=ON -DEXR_ENABLE_LIBRARY=ON -DEXR_ENABLE_AUDIO=ON -DEXR_ENABLE_PDF=ON \
+            -DEXR_ENABLE_PDF_EDIT=OFF -DEXR_ENABLE_PDF_CRYPTO=OFF -DEXR_ENABLE_AAC=OFF \
+            -DEXR_ENABLE_TEXT_INDEX=OFF -DEXR_BUILD_TEXT_INDEX=OFF -DEXR_ENABLE_IMAGE_WEBP=OFF \
+            -DEXR_ENABLE_IMAGE_TIFF=OFF -DEXR_ENABLE_IMAGE_AVIF=OFF -DEXR_BUILD_SHARED_COMPAT=ON \
+            2>&1 | tee "$ws/logs/sdk-configure.log"
+          cmake --build "$ws/sdk" --parallel 4 --target install 2>&1 | tee "$ws/logs/sdk-build.log"
+          test -f "$ws/sdk-stage/lib/libexreader_jni.so"
+      - name: Install the raw0.2 product and reviewed wave3 packages into one prefix
+        run: |
+          set -o pipefail
+          ws="$PWD/build/wave3"
+          stage="$ws/stage"
+          sdk="$ws/sdk-stage"
+          mkdir -p "$ws/logs"
+          build_module() {
+            name="$1"; source="$2"; shift 2
+            cmake -S "$source" -B "$ws/module-$name" -G Ninja -DCMAKE_TOOLCHAIN_FILE="$C11_TOOLCHAIN" \
+              -DANDROID_ABI=x86_64 -DANDROID_PLATFORM=android-26 -DANDROID_STL=c++_static \
+              -DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Release -DCMAKE_POSITION_INDEPENDENT_CODE=ON \

+              2>&1 | tee "$ws/logs/$name-configure.log"
+            cmake --build "$ws/module-$name" --parallel 4 --target install 2>&1 | tee "$ws/logs/$name-build.log"
+          }

+name: Common source Apple three-target native feature integration
+on:
+  push:
+    branches: [codex/reader-same-source-integration]
+permissions:
+  contents: read
+  actions: read
+env:
+  EXR_APPLE_SDK_VERSION: '3.41.0'
+  EXR_APPLE_PAGE_THEME: 'ON'
+  EXR_APPLE_RAW3: 'OFF'
+jobs:
+  sdk:
+    name: Team24 Apple installed ${{ matrix.target }}
+    runs-on: macos-15
+    timeout-minutes: 55
+    strategy:
+      fail-fast: false
+      matrix:
+        target: [macos, iphonesimulator]
+    steps:
+      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
+        with:
+          fetch-depth: 0
+      - name: Admit common source and exact Apple shared checker
+        run: |
+          python3 tests/reader_same_source/guard.py --source "$GITHUB_SHA" --report build/apple-source-admission.json
+          test "$(git rev-parse HEAD:tools/check_apple_product.py)" = ed7f62597e01d4e045f679369805d27abc20b7bc
+      - name: Checkout isolated Apple owner candidate with full history
+        uses: actions/checkout@v4
+        with:
+          ref: 937f61057b06c5c9de3b562046e52c91b5be62b9
+          path: apple-owner-candidate
+          fetch-depth: 0
+      - name: Original strict Apple owner guard on isolated candidate
+        working-directory: apple-owner-candidate
+        run: GITHUB_SHA=937f61057b06c5c9de3b562046e52c91b5be62b9 python3 tests/reader_team24_apple/guard_owner.py
+      - name: Original Apple syntax checks on common source
+        run: |
+          python3 -m compileall -q tests/reader_team24_apple tests/reader_team24_apple/build.py
+          ruby -c tests/reader_team24_apple/make_project.rb
+      - name: Frozen SDK and exact installed five module family
+        run: python3 tests/reader_team24_apple/build.py --target '${{ matrix.target }}'
+      - name: Actual account session terminal callbacks
+        if: ${{ matrix.target == 'macos' }}
+        run: |
+          mkdir -p build/account-terminal
+          sdk="$PWD/build/raw3-ios-sdk/macos/sdk"
+          product="$PWD/build/raw3-ios-sdk/macos/raw-products"
+          python3 - "$sdk" "$product" <<'PY'
+          import subprocess, sys
+          sys.path.insert(0, 'tools')
+          from apple_swift import flags
+          sdk, product = sys.argv[1:]
+          sources = ['examples/apple/HostSDK.swift', 'examples/apple/HostCatalog.swift',
+                     'examples/apple/HostAccount.swift', 'tests/reader_team24_apple/AccountTerminalReview.swift']

+                          '-Xlinker', '-rpath', '-Xlinker', sdk + '/lib', *sources,
+                          '-o', 'build/account-terminal/account-terminal', *flags(sdk, product, True)], check=True)
+          PY
+          python3 tests/reader_team24_apple/run_account_terminal.py

+          name: team24-apple-sdk-${{ matrix.target }}-${{ github.run_id }}-${{ github.run_attempt }}

+name: GTK raw retained navigation and folder import
+on:
+  push:
+    branches: [codex/reader-same-source-integration]
+permissions:
+  contents: read
+jobs:
+  retained:
+    runs-on: ubuntu-22.04
+    timeout-minutes: 150
+    steps:
+      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
+        with:
+          fetch-depth: 0
+      - name: Admit exact integration source
+        run: python3 tests/reader_same_source/guard.py --source "$GITHUB_SHA" --report build/gtk-source-admission.json
+      - name: Install GTK dependencies
+        run: |
+          sudo apt-get update
+          sudo apt-get install -y libglib2.0-dev libgtk-3-dev libsecret-1-dev gnome-keyring libcurl4-openssl-dev libxml2-dev libssl-dev pkg-config at-spi2-core libatk-adaptor python3-gi gir1.2-atspi-2.0 xvfb openbox xdotool xclip x11-utils wmctrl imagemagick python3-pil xdg-desktop-portal xdg-desktop-portal-gtk gsettings-desktop-schemas dbus-x11 ninja-build
+      - name: Build installed raw family and real packaged host
+        run: |
+          python3 -m py_compile tests/reader_gtk_raw_wave3/run.py tests/reader_gtk_navigation/*.py tests/reader_gtk_batch_import/*.py
+          python3 - <<'PY'
+          import importlib.util
+          spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('raw', 'tests/reader_gtk_raw_wave3/run.py')
+          raw = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
+          spec.loader.exec_module(raw)
+          raw.build_sdk()
+          raw.install_family('raw', raw.PREFIX, True)
+          assert raw.installed('raw', raw.PREFIX)['accepted']
+          raw.cmake('raw-batch-import', raw.ROOT / 'apps/reader/batch-import', raw.OUT / 'raw-batch-import', raw.PREFIX, install=True)
+          raw.cmake('raw-preferences', raw.ROOT / 'apps/reader/preferences', raw.OUT / 'raw-preferences', raw.PREFIX, install=True)
+          options = raw.host_options(raw.PREFIX) + ['-DEXR_READER_GTK_RAW3=ON', '-DREADER_GTK_RAW3_PREFIX=' + str(raw.PREFIX), '-DEXR_READER_GTK_BATCH_IMPORT=ON', '-DREADER_BATCH_IMPORT_PREFIX=' + str(raw.PREFIX), '-DEXR_READER_GTK_PREFERENCES=ON', '-DREADER_PREFERENCES_PREFIX=' + str(raw.PREFIX)]

+name: Android shelf retained cloud evidence inspection
+on:
+  push:
+    branches: [codex/reader-shelf-inspect]
+    paths: ['.github/workflows/reader-shelf-inspect.yml']
+  workflow_dispatch:
+permissions:
+  contents: read
+  actions: read
+jobs:
+  inspect:
+    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
+    timeout-minutes: 8
+    steps:
+      - name: Read fixed failed UI evidence on this cloud runner
+        env:
+          GH_TOKEN: ${{ github.token }}
+        run: |
+          python3 - <<'PY'
+          import hashlib,io,json,os,urllib.request,urllib.error,urllib.parse,zipfile
+          from pathlib import Path,PurePosixPath
+          API='https://api.github.com/repos/NoGod3524/EXReader'
+          RUN=40034076410
+          SOURCE='23e5133fcf06a327605d00b893072ff36d39e0e1'
+          ARTIFACT=12536608131
+          SIZE=106901651
+          DIGEST='sha256:b5024d1d8006c6076ac660073cacab012571afe38781cd03ccee42caeb79e34b'
+          headers={'Authorization':'Bearer '+os.environ['GH_TOKEN'],'Accept':'application/vnd.github+json','User-Agent':'EXReader-shelf-cloud-inspection'}
+          def require(value,message):
+              if not value:raise ValueError(message)
+          def api(path):
+              with urllib.request.urlopen(urllib.request.Request(API+path,headers=headers),timeout=60) as stream:return json.load(stream)
+          run=api('/actions/runs/'+str(RUN));metadata=api('/actions/artifacts/'+str(ARTIFACT))
+          require(run['head_sha']==SOURCE and run['status']=='completed' and run['conclusion']=='failure','failed-run identity differs')
+          require(metadata['workflow_run']['id']==RUN and metadata['workflow_run']['head_sha']==SOURCE and metadata['size_in_bytes']==SIZE
+                  and metadata['digest']==DIGEST and not metadata['expired'],'retained artifact identity differs')
+          class NoRedirect(urllib.request.HTTPRedirectHandler):
+              def redirect_request(self,request,stream,code,message,headers,url):return None
+          request=urllib.request.Request(API+'/actions/artifacts/'+str(ARTIFACT)+'/zip',headers=headers)
+          try:stream=urllib.request.build_opener(NoRedirect).open(request,timeout=60)
+          except urllib.error.HTTPError as error:
+              require(error.code==302,'signed artifact redirect missing')
+              location=error.headers['Location'];target=urllib.parse.urlparse(location)
+              require(target.scheme=='https' and target.hostname and not target.username and not target.password,'invalid artifact redirect')
+              stream=urllib.request.urlopen(location,timeout=60)
+          with stream:raw=stream.read(SIZE+1)
+          require(len(raw)==SIZE and 'sha256:'+hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()==DIGEST,'ZIP digest differs')
+          archive=zipfile.ZipFile(io.BytesIO(raw));names=archive.namelist()
+          require(len(names)==len(set(names)) and sum(item.file_size for item in archive.infolist())<1024**3,'invalid ZIP entries')
+          for name in names:
+              path=PurePosixPath(name);require(not path.is_absolute() and '..' not in path.parts and '\\' not in name,'unsafe ZIP path')
+          def read(suffix):
+              found=[name for name in names if name.endswith(suffix)];require(len(found)==1,'missing/ambiguous '+suffix)
+              return archive.read(found[0])
+          value=json.loads(read('/ui-execution/ui/validation.json'))
+          require(value['source_sha']==SOURCE and value['run_id']==str(RUN) and value['attempt']=='1','UI source/run differs')
+          report={'diagnostic_only':True,'accepted':False,'source_sha':SOURCE,'run_id':RUN,'artifact_id':ARTIFACT,'digest':DIGEST,
+                  'passed':value['passed'],'failed':value['failed'],'skipped':value['skipped'],'cases':[]}
+          for case in value['cases']:
+              item={'id':case['id'],'status':case['status'],'error':case.get('error'),'path':'ui-execution/ui/'+case['id']+'.json'}
+              if case['status']=='fail':item['detail']=case.get('detail',{})
+              report['cases'].append(item)
+          report['capture_result']=json.loads(read('/ui-execution/result.json'))
+          report['original_suites']={}
+          for suite,folder in [('walkthrough','device'),('review','review'),('bounded','bounded')]:
+              value=json.loads(read('/evidence/'+folder+'/validation.json'))
+              report['original_suites'][suite]={'summary':value.get('summary'),'sequence':value.get('sequence'),
+                  'failed_cases':[{key:case.get(key) for key in ('id','status','error','detail','assertions')} for case in value['cases'] if case.get('status')!='pass']}
+          capture_errors=[]
+          for name in names:
+              if '/ui-execution/windows/' in name and name.endswith('.jsonl'):
+                  for line in archive.read(name).splitlines():
+                      row=json.loads(line)
+                      if row['kind']=='error':capture_errors.append({'path':name,'detail':row.get('detail')})
+          report['capture_errors']=capture_errors
+          output=Path('build/shelf-inspection');output.mkdir(parents=True)
+          (output/'inspection.json').write_text(json.dumps(report,indent=2)+'\n')
+          print(json.dumps(report),flush=True)
+          PY
+      - uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
+        if: always()
+        with:
+          name: reader-shelf-inspection-${{ github.run_id }}-${{ github.run_attempt }}
+          path: build/shelf-inspection/
+          if-no-files-found: error

+name: Reader account sync fault boundaries
+on:
+  push:
+    branches: [codex/reader-sync-resilience]
+  workflow_dispatch:
+permissions:
+  contents: read
+defaults:
+  run:
+    shell: bash
+jobs:
+  boundaries:
+    name: fault boundaries (${{ matrix.os }})
+    runs-on: ${{ matrix.os }}
+    timeout-minutes: 70
+    strategy:
+      fail-fast: false
+      matrix:
+        os: [ubuntu-22.04, windows-2022, macos-15]
+    steps:
+      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
+        with:
+          fetch-depth: 0
+      - name: Require fixed start, frozen SDK, product schema and package path ownership
+        run: |
+          FIXED=f43baf9182d75907c0cb5d6f77048f27bca8c950
+          K=9854061be331fe603a1822d9b2b36ee357154927
+          test "$(git rev-parse HEAD)" = "$GITHUB_SHA"
+          git merge-base --is-ancestor "$FIXED" HEAD
+          git diff --exit-code "$K" HEAD -- src include cmake CMakeLists.txt tests/fixtures .github/workflows/native.yml .github/workflows/reliability.yml bindings
+          git diff --exit-code "$FIXED" HEAD -- apps/reader/store apps/reader/session apps/reader/sync apps/reader/revisions extras/webdav extras/opds
+          allowed='^(tests/reader_sync_resilience/|\.github/workflows/reader-sync-resilience\.yml$|docs/READER-SYNC-RESILIENCE\.md$|docs/validation/reader-sync-resilience\.json$)'
+          changed=$(git diff --name-only "$FIXED" HEAD)
+          bad=$(printf '%s\n' "$changed" | sed '/^$/d' | grep -Ev "$allowed" || true)
+          test -z "$bad" || { echo "unowned path: $bad"; exit 1; }
+      - name: Install Linux native TLS XML and SHA dependencies
+        if: runner.os == 'Linux'
+        run: |
+          sudo apt-get update
+          sudo apt-get install -y libcurl4-openssl-dev libxml2-dev libssl-dev pkg-config ninja-build
+      - name: Use Apple system native TLS XML and platform SHA with fixture-only OpenSSL CLI
+        if: runner.os == 'macOS'
+        run: |
+          brew install ninja pkg-config openssl@3
+          sdkroot=$(xcrun --sdk macosx --show-sdk-path)
+          mkdir -p build/apple-pkgconfig
+          version=$(/usr/bin/curl --version | awk 'NR==1 {print $2}')
+          cat > build/apple-pkgconfig/libcurl.pc <<EOF
+          prefix=$sdkroot/usr
+          Name: libcurl
+          Description: Apple SDK system libcurl
+          Version: $version
+          Libs: -L\${prefix}/lib -lcurl
+          Cflags:
+          EOF
+          echo "PKG_CONFIG_PATH=$PWD/build/apple-pkgconfig" >> "$GITHUB_ENV"
+          echo "CMAKE_PREFIX_PATH=$sdkroot/usr" >> "$GITHUB_ENV"
+          echo "PATH=$(brew --prefix openssl@3)/bin:$PATH" >> "$GITHUB_ENV"
+          /usr/bin/curl --version
+      - name: Enter the native Windows compiler environment
+        if: runner.os == 'Windows'
+        shell: pwsh
+        run: |
+          $vs = & "${env:ProgramFiles(x86)}\Microsoft Visual Studio\Installer\vswhere.exe" -latest -products * -requires Microsoft.VisualStudio.Component.VC.Tools.x86.x64 -property installationPath
+          $bat = Join-Path $vs 'VC\Auxiliary\Build\vcvars64.bat'
+          cmd /c "`"$bat`" >nul && set" | ForEach-Object {
+            if ($_ -match '^([^=]+)=(.*)$') { "$($Matches[1])=$($Matches[2])" | Out-File $env:GITHUB_ENV -Append -Encoding utf8 }
+          }
+          'CC=cl' | Out-File $env:GITHUB_ENV -Append -Encoding utf8
+          'CXX=cl' | Out-File $env:GITHUB_ENV -Append -Encoding utf8
+      - name: Build and install the unchanged native product, TLS, revision, sync and session modules
+        run: |
+          mkdir -p build/resilience-logs
+          if [ "$RUNNER_OS" = Windows ]; then ws=$(cygpath -m "$PWD"); else ws="$PWD"; fi
+          echo "RES_PREFIXES=$ws/build/res-dav-stage;$ws/build/res-revision-stage;$ws/build/res-product-stage;$ws/build/res-sync-stage" >> "$GITHUB_ENV"
+          echo "RES_SESSION_STAGE=$ws/build/res-session-stage" >> "$GITHUB_ENV"
+          cmake -S apps/reader/store/native -B build/res-product -G Ninja -DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Release -DCMAKE_INSTALL_PREFIX="$ws/build/res-product-stage" 2>&1 | tee build/resilience-logs/product-configure.log
+          cmake --build build/res-product --verbose 2>&1 | tee build/resilience-logs/product-build.log
+          cmake --install build/res-product 2>&1 | tee build/resilience-logs/product-install.log
+          cmake -S extras/webdav -B build/res-dav -G Ninja -DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Release -DCMAKE_INSTALL_PREFIX="$ws/build/res-dav-stage" 2>&1 | tee build/resilience-logs/dav-configure.log
+          cmake --build build/res-dav --verbose 2>&1 | tee build/resilience-logs/dav-build.log
+          cmake --install build/res-dav 2>&1 | tee build/resilience-logs/dav-install.log
+          cmake -S apps/reader/revisions -B build/res-revisions -G Ninja -DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Release -DREADER_PRODUCT_PREFIX="$ws/build/res-product-stage" -DCMAKE_INSTALL_PREFIX="$ws/build/res-revision-stage" 2>&1 | tee build/resilience-logs/revision-configure.log
+          cmake --build build/res-revisions --verbose 2>&1 | tee build/resilience-logs/revision-build.log
+          cmake --install build/res-revisions 2>&1 | tee build/resilience-logs/revision-install.log
+          cmake -S apps/reader/sync -B build/res-sync -G Ninja -DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Release -DCMAKE_PREFIX_PATH="$ws/build/res-dav-stage;$ws/build/res-revision-stage;$ws/build/res-product-stage" -DCMAKE_INSTALL_PREFIX="$ws/build/res-sync-stage" 2>&1 | tee build/resilience-logs/sync-configure.log
+          cmake --build build/res-sync --verbose 2>&1 | tee build/resilience-logs/sync-build.log
+          cmake --install build/res-sync 2>&1 | tee build/resilience-logs/sync-install.log
+          cmake -S apps/reader/session -B build/res-session -G Ninja -DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Release -DCMAKE_PREFIX_PATH="$ws/build/res-sync-stage;$ws/build/res-dav-stage;$ws/build/res-revision-stage;$ws/build/res-product-stage" -DCMAKE_INSTALL_PREFIX="$ws/build/res-session-stage" 2>&1 | tee build/resilience-logs/session-configure.log
+          cmake --build build/res-session --verbose 2>&1 | tee build/resilience-logs/session-build.log
+          cmake --install build/res-session 2>&1 | tee build/resilience-logs/session-install.log
+      - name: Build the installed-package consumers
+        run: |
+          cmake -S tests/reader_revisions/consumer -B build/res-revision-consumer -G Ninja -DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Release -DCMAKE_PREFIX_PATH="$RES_PREFIXES" 2>&1 | tee build/resilience-logs/revision-consumer-configure.log
+          cmake --build build/res-revision-consumer --verbose 2>&1 | tee build/resilience-logs/revision-consumer-build.log
+          cmake -S tests/reader_session/consumer -B build/res-session-consumer -G Ninja -DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Release -DCMAKE_PREFIX_PATH="$RES_SESSION_STAGE;$RES_PREFIXES" 2>&1 | tee build/resilience-logs/session-consumer-configure.log
+          cmake --build build/res-session-consumer --verbose 2>&1 | tee build/resilience-logs/session-consumer-build.log
+          cmake -S tests/reader_sync_resilience/consumer -B build/res-resilience-consumer -G Ninja -DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Release -DCMAKE_PREFIX_PATH="$RES_SESSION_STAGE;$RES_PREFIXES" 2>&1 | tee build/resilience-logs/resilience-consumer-configure.log
+          cmake --build build/res-resilience-consumer --verbose 2>&1 | tee build/resilience-logs/resilience-consumer-build.log
+      - name: Preserve the unchanged 20-item account-session acceptance
+        run: |
+          if [ "$RUNNER_OS" = Windows ]; then exe=.exe; else exe=; fi
+          python3 tests/reader_session/check_session.py --consumer "build/res-session-consumer/session-consumer$exe" --revision-consumer "build/res-revision-consumer/revision-consumer$exe" --output build/session-evidence
+      - name: Real forced termination and abrupt exit at named fault boundaries
+        run: |
+          if [ "$RUNNER_OS" = Windows ]; then exe=.exe; else exe=; fi
+          python3 tests/reader_sync_resilience/check_resilience.py --consumer "build/res-resilience-consumer/resilience-consumer$exe" --revision-consumer "build/res-revision-consumer/revision-consumer$exe" --original-report build/session-evidence/result.json --output build/resilience-evidence
+      - name: Preserve source-bound evidence without private keys
+        if: always()
+        uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
+        with:
+          name: reader-sync-resilience-${{ runner.os }}-${{ github.run_id }}-${{ github.run_attempt }}
+          path: |
+            build/resilience-logs/
+            build/resilience-evidence/
+            build/session-evidence/
+            build/res-session-consumer/session-consumer*
+            build/res-resilience-consumer/resilience-consumer*
+            build/res-revision-consumer/revision-consumer*
+            !build/**/certificates/*.key

#!/usr/bin/env python3
# SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
"""Check SDK identity and exact, evidence-bound historical literal exceptions."""
import argparse
from functools import lru_cache
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path
import re
import subprocess
import sys

from sdk_version import ROOT, source_version

MANIFEST = 'tools/version_literal_exceptions.json'
LITERAL = re.compile(r'(?<![0-9])3\.4[0-9]\.0(?![0-9])')
CONSUMER_VERSION = re.compile(r'find_package\s*\(\s*EXReader\s+"?(\d+(?:\.\d+){0,3})"?(?=[\s)])', re.I)


def sha(data):
    return hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()


@lru_cache(maxsize=512)
def object_bytes(root, commit, path):
    return subprocess.check_output(['git', '-C', str(root), 'show', commit + ':' + path])


def tracked(root):
    return subprocess.check_output(['git', '-C', str(root), 'ls-files', '--cached', '--others', '--exclude-standard'], text=True).splitlines()


def check(root):
    version = source_version(root)
    errors = []

    def error(path, line, actual, expected):
        errors.append(f'{path}:{line} -> {actual} vs expected {expected}')

    def read(path):
        if Path(path).is_absolute() or '\\' in path or '..' in Path(path).parts:
            raise ValueError('exception references must use portable repository paths')
        target = (root / path).resolve()
        if not target.is_relative_to(root.resolve()):
            raise ValueError('exception path escapes repository')
        return target.read_text(encoding='utf-8').splitlines()

    def reference(ref):
        lines = read(ref['file'])
        first, last = ref['line'], ref.get('end_line', ref['line'])
        if not (1 <= first <= last <= len(lines)):
            raise ValueError('missing evidence lines')
        text = '\n'.join(lines[first - 1:last])
        if sha(text.encode()) != ref['sha256']:
            raise ValueError('evidence bytes changed')
        if 'file_sha256' in ref and sha((root / ref['file']).read_bytes()) != ref['file_sha256']:
            raise ValueError('test/workflow evidence file changed')
        return text

    source = (root / 'cmake/versions.cmake').read_text(encoding='utf-8')
    abi = re.search(r'set\(EXR_ABI_VERSION\s+(\d+)\)', source)[1]
    template = (root / 'cmake/version.h.in').read_text(encoding='utf-8')
    expected = template.replace('@EXR_SDK_VERSION@', version).replace('@EXR_ABI_VERSION@', abi)
    if (root / 'include/exr/version.h').read_bytes() != expected.encode('utf-8'):
        error('include/exr/version.h', 1, 'generated header differs', f'SDK {version}, ABI {abi}; run --sync-generated')
    cmake = read('CMakeLists.txt')
    project = next((i for i, line in enumerate(cmake, 1) if re.search(r'^project\(EXReader\b', line)), 0)
    if not project or cmake[project - 1] != 'project(EXReader VERSION ${EXR_SDK_VERSION} LANGUAGES C)':
        error('CMakeLists.txt', project or 1, cmake[project - 1] if project else 'missing project()', version + ' from ${EXR_SDK_VERSION}')
    if 'include(cmake/versions.cmake)' not in cmake[:max(0, project - 1)]:
        error('CMakeLists.txt', 1, 'missing include before project', 'cmake/versions.cmake')
    for number, line in enumerate(cmake, 1):
        if number != project and LITERAL.search(line):
            error('CMakeLists.txt', number, line, 'version variables from cmake/versions.cmake, not bare literals')
    base = read('include/exr/base.h')
    if '#define EXR_VERSION EXR_SDK_VERSION' not in base or '#include <exr/version.h>' not in base:
        error('include/exr/base.h', 1, 'SDK macro does not use generated header', version)
    if 'Version: @PROJECT_VERSION@' not in read('cmake/exreader.pc.in'):
        error('cmake/exreader.pc.in', 1, 'pkg-config version does not use project identity', version)
    if not re.search(r'write_basic_package_version_file\([^\n]*VERSION\s+\$\{PROJECT_VERSION\}', '\n'.join(cmake)):
        error('CMakeLists.txt', 1, 'package version does not use project identity', version)
    for i, line in enumerate(read('README.md'), 1):
        if '???' in line:
            found = re.search(r'(\d+\.\d+\.\d+) ???', line)
            if not found or found[1] != version:
                error('README.md', i, found[1] if found else line, version)
            break
    else:
        error('README.md', 1, 'missing development version line', version)

#!/usr/bin/env python3
# SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
"""Read current SDK identity or an installed, possibly historical, SDK header."""
from pathlib import Path
import re

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]


def source_version(root=ROOT):
    text = (Path(root) / 'cmake/versions.cmake').read_text(encoding='utf-8')
    return re.search(r'set\(EXR_SDK_VERSION\s+([0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+)\)', text)[1]


def header_text(path):
    """Retain the legacy public macro for inspectors; resolve its generated alias."""
    path = Path(path)
    text = path.read_text(encoding='utf-8')
    if re.search(r'^#define EXR_VERSION EXR_SDK_VERSION$', text, re.M):
        generated = path.with_name('version.h').read_text(encoding='utf-8')
        version = re.search(r'^#define EXR_SDK_VERSION "([0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+)"$', generated, re.M)[1]
        text = text.replace('#define EXR_VERSION EXR_SDK_VERSION', f'#define EXR_VERSION "{version}"')
        text += '\n' + generated
    return text


def installed_version(sdk):
    text = header_text(Path(sdk) / 'include/exr/base.h')
    return re.search(r'^#define EXR_VERSION "([0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+)"$', text, re.M)[1]


if __name__ == '__main__':
    print(source_version())

def sync(root):
    source = (root / 'cmake/versions.cmake').read_text(encoding='utf-8')
    version = source_version(root)
    abi = re.search(r'set\(EXR_ABI_VERSION\s+(\d+)\)', source)[1]
    header = (root / 'cmake/version.h.in').read_text(encoding='utf-8').replace('@EXR_SDK_VERSION@', version).replace('@EXR_ABI_VERSION@', abi)
    (root / 'include/exr/version.h').write_text(header, encoding='utf-8', newline='\n')
    readme = root / 'README.md'
    text = re.sub(r'\d+\.\d+\.\d+(?= ???)', version, readme.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
    readme.write_text(text, encoding='utf-8', newline='\n')


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument('--sync-generated', action='store_true')
    parser.add_argument('--self-test', action='store_true')
    args = parser.parse_args()
    if args.sync_generated:
        sync(ROOT)
    if args.self_test:
        if check(ROOT):
            return 1
        path = ROOT / 'CMakeLists.txt'
        original = path.read_bytes()
        version = source_version()
        major, minor, patch = map(int, version.split('.'))
        wrong = f'{major}.{minor - 1}.{patch}'
        try:
            path.write_text(original.decode().replace('${EXR_SDK_VERSION}', wrong, 1), encoding='utf-8', newline='\n')
            result = subprocess.run([sys.executable, __file__], text=True, capture_output=True)
            print(result.stdout, end='')
            if result.returncode != 1 or 'CMakeLists.txt:3' not in result.stdout or version not in result.stdout:
                raise AssertionError('wrong version was not rejected at exact location')
            print('PASS: deliberate version drift rejected (exit 1)')
        finally:
            path.write_bytes(original)
        path = ROOT / 'tests/reading_corpus/CMakeLists.txt'
        original = path.read_bytes()
        try:
            path.write_text(original.decode().replace('find_package(EXReader CONFIG', f'find_package(EXReader {wrong} EXACT CONFIG', 1), encoding='utf-8', newline='\n')
            result = subprocess.run([sys.executable, __file__], text=True, capture_output=True, encoding='utf-8', errors='replace')
            print(result.stdout, end='')
            expected = f'tests/reading_corpus/CMakeLists.txt:3 -> {wrong} vs expected {version}; ??????? version file,?????'
            if result.returncode != 1 or expected not in result.stdout:
                raise AssertionError('wrong consumer literal was not rejected with version-file guidance')
            print('PASS: deliberate consumer version drift rejected (exit 1)')
        finally:
            path.write_bytes(original)
        return 0
    return int(check(ROOT))


if __name__ == '__main__':
    raise SystemExit(main())

+static int shell_contrast;
+static unsigned shell_contrast_generation;
+static uint32_t shell_sys_rgb(int index) { DWORD c=GetSysColor(index); return ((c&255u)<<16)|(c&0xff00u)|((c>>16)&255u); }
+static void shell_contrast_refresh(void) {
+  HIGHCONTRASTW high; memset(&high,0,sizeof(high)); high.cbSize=sizeof(high);
+  shell_contrast=SystemParametersInfoW(SPI_GETHIGHCONTRAST,sizeof(high),&high,0) && (high.dwFlags&HCF_HIGHCONTRASTON);
+  if(!shell_contrast) return;

+/* SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT */
+/* ?? (OPDS catalogs): browse a remote library, download a book into the local
+   shelf. Parsing is exr_opds_*; transport is opds_net.h (WinHTTP, HTTPS only,
+   10 minute feed cache). All network work runs on one worker thread; results
+   come back to the UI thread as a window message. A downloaded file goes
+   through the normal import job, so metadata, cover and reading are the same
+   as for a local book. */
+#include <exr/opds.h>
+#include "opds_net.h"
+enum {
+  OPDS_ID_BACK=300,OPDS_ID_NEXT,OPDS_ID_REFRESH,OPDS_ID_GO,OPDS_ID_SEARCH,OPDS_ID_ACTION,OPDS_ID_CANCEL,OPDS_ID_CLOSE,
+  OPDS_ID_URL,OPDS_ID_QUERY,OPDS_ID_LIST,OPDS_ID_URL_LABEL,OPDS_ID_QUERY_LABEL,
+  OPDS_MSG_DONE=WM_APP+0x210,OPDS_MSG_PROGRESS,OPDS_STACK=24
+};
+typedef enum { OPDS_OP_FEED=1,OPDS_OP_SEARCH,OPDS_OP_DOWNLOAD } opds_op;
+typedef struct {
+  opds_op kind;
+  char url[2048],query[256],search_template[2048],search_description[2048];
+  int use_cache,nav; /* nav: 1 push current page, -1 pop, 2 new source (reset history), 0 stay */
+  char title[256],format[16];
+  wchar_t dest[HOST_PATH];
+  unsigned receive_ms;
+  exr_opds_feed feed;int has_feed;
+  char found_template[2048],feed_url[2048];
+  onet_result result;
+  char error[256];
+} opds_job;
+static HWND opds_view,opds_url_edit,opds_query_edit,opds_list;
+static exr_opds_feed opds_feed;
+static char opds_url[2048],opds_stack[OPDS_STACK][2048],opds_search_template[2048],opds_search_description[2048];
+static int opds_depth,opds_visible;
+static volatile LONG opds_cancel;
+static volatile ULONGLONG opds_done_bytes,opds_total_bytes;
+static ULONGLONG opds_last_progress_tick;
+static wchar_t opds_status[512],opds_download_path[HOST_PATH],opds_download_title[256],opds_source_file[HOST_PATH];
+static onet_result opds_last_result;
+static unsigned opds_receive_override,opds_downloads_done,opds_downloads_cancelled,opds_downloads_failed,opds_imports_started,opds_feeds_loaded;
+static int opds_last_op,opds_last_from_cache;
+static HBRUSH opds_brush;
+static WNDPROC opds_edit_base;
+
+static void opds_set_status(const wchar_t *text) {
+  wcsncpy(opds_status,text,511);opds_status[511]=0;
+  if(opds_view)InvalidateRect(opds_view,NULL,FALSE);
+}
+static void opds_set_status_utf8(const char *prefix,const char *text) {
+  wchar_t *a=host_wide(prefix,strlen(prefix)),*b=host_wide(text,strlen(text));
+  wchar_t line[512];swprintf(line,512,L"%ls%ls",a ? a:L"",b ? b:L"");free(a);free(b);
+  opds_set_status(line);
+}
+static void opds_controls_enable(void) {
+  int busy=opds_busy!=0,selected=opds_list ? (int)SendMessageW(opds_list,LB_GETCURSEL,0,0):-1;
+  EnableWindow(GetDlgItem(opds_view,OPDS_ID_GO),!busy);EnableWindow(GetDlgItem(opds_view,OPDS_ID_SEARCH),!busy);
+  EnableWindow(GetDlgItem(opds_view,OPDS_ID_BACK),!busy && opds_depth>0);
+  EnableWindow(GetDlgItem(opds_view,OPDS_ID_NEXT),!busy && opds_feed.next_url!=NULL);
+  EnableWindow(GetDlgItem(opds_view,OPDS_ID_REFRESH),!busy && opds_url[0]);
+  EnableWindow(GetDlgItem(opds_view,OPDS_ID_ACTION),!busy && selected>=0 && (size_t)selected<opds_feed.entry_count);
+  EnableWindow(GetDlgItem(opds_view,OPDS_ID_CANCEL),busy);

static int shell_system_dark(void) {
  DWORD value=1,bytes=sizeof(value);
  RegGetValueW(HKEY_CURRENT_USER,L"Software\\Microsoft\\Windows\\CurrentVersion\\Themes\\Personalize",
    L"AppsUseLightTheme",RRF_RT_REG_DWORD,NULL,&value,&bytes);
  return value==0;
}
/* Windows high contrast wins over the light/dark choice: the palette is the
   system colours, never the design tokens. 2 is the `dark` value that selects it. */
static int shell_contrast;
static unsigned shell_contrast_generation;
static uint32_t shell_sys_rgb(int index) { DWORD c=GetSysColor(index); return ((c&255u)<<16)|(c&0xff00u)|((c>>16)&255u); }
static void shell_contrast_refresh(void) {
  HIGHCONTRASTW high; memset(&high,0,sizeof(high)); high.cbSize=sizeof(high);
  shell_contrast=SystemParametersInfoW(SPI_GETHIGHCONTRAST,sizeof(high),&high,0) && (high.dwFlags&HCF_HIGHCONTRASTON);
  if(!shell_contrast) return;
  shell_theme_contrast=(shell_theme){
    shell_sys_rgb(COLOR_WINDOW),shell_sys_rgb(COLOR_WINDOW),shell_sys_rgb(COLOR_WINDOW),
    shell_sys_rgb(COLOR_BTNFACE),shell_sys_rgb(COLOR_BTNFACE),shell_sys_rgb(COLOR_WINDOW),
    shell_sys_rgb(COLOR_WINDOWTEXT),shell_sys_rgb(COLOR_WINDOWTEXT),shell_sys_rgb(COLOR_GRAYTEXT),
    shell_sys_rgb(COLOR_HOTLIGHT),shell_sys_rgb(COLOR_HIGHLIGHT),shell_sys_rgb(COLOR_HIGHLIGHTTEXT),
    shell_sys_rgb(COLOR_WINDOWTEXT)};
  shell_contrast_generation++;
}

  if(shell_contrast) return 2;
  return shell_theme_mode==1 || (shell_theme_mode==2 && shell_system_dark());

on:
  push:
    branches: ['codex/framework']
  pull_request:
  workflow_call:
  workflow_dispatch:
    inputs:
      scope:
        description: Run the full workflow or only P acceptance jobs
        type: choice
        options: [full, p-hygiene, p-apple, p-windows]
        default: full
      fault:
        description: P acceptance only - mutate one temporary workflow copy
        type: choice
        options: [none, packaging, failfast, diagnostic, negative, baseline-new, baseline-modified, baseline-stale]
        default: none
      rule:
        description: P hygiene rule to run (use all for the positive control)
        type: choice
        options: [all, packaging, failfast, diagnostic, negative]
        default: all
permissions:
  contents: read
jobs:
  desktop:
    if: github.event_name != 'workflow_dispatch' || inputs.scope == 'full'

          "$test" tests/fixtures --font "$font"

        shell: bash .github/scripts/ci.sh {0}

# SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
# Independent consumer build for the Windows reference host.
#
# examples/CMakeLists.txt is owned by the integration branch, so this project
# consumes the installed SDK directly and reuses ../reader.c without touching
# the shared example entry point. It also works from the installed tree
# (stage/examples/windows).
cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.24)
project(EXReaderWindowsHost LANGUAGES C)
set(CMAKE_C_STANDARD 11)
set(CMAKE_C_STANDARD_REQUIRED ON)
if(NOT EXR_SDK)
  message(FATAL_ERROR "Set -DEXR_SDK=<installed SDK prefix>")
endif()

  set(CMAKE_MSVC_RUNTIME_LIBRARY "MultiThreaded$<$<CONFIG:Debug>:Debug>")
  add_compile_options(/utf-8)
endif()

endif()

if(NOT MSVC)
  target_link_libraries(EXReader PRIVATE m)
endif()

 * progress restore and a UI-level acceptance driver. The original 26-check
 * --self-test path is kept below for the packaging tool.
 *

 * examples/CMakeLists.txt is owned by the integration branch. examples/windows
 * contains an independent consumer build against the installed SDK.
 */

def fetch(item):
    name,url,expected=item
    archive=root/(name+('.zip' if url.endswith('.zip') else '.tar.gz'))
    if not archive.exists() or hashlib.sha256(archive.read_bytes()).hexdigest()"'!=expected:
        subprocess.run(['"'C:/Windows/System32/curl.exe','--fail','--location','--retry','2','--max-time','180','--silent','--show-error','--output',str(archive),url],check=True)
    assert hashlib.sha256(archive.read_bytes()).hexdigest()==expected,name
    dest=root/name
    dest.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    if archive.suffix=='.zip':
        with zipfile.ZipFile(archive) as f: f.extractall(dest)
    else:
        with tarfile.open(archive) as f: f.extractall(dest,filter='data')
    dirs=[x for x in dest.iterdir() if x.is_dir()]
    assert len(dirs)==1,(name,dirs)
    print(name+' verified '+str(dirs[0]),flush=True)
    return name,str(dirs[0]).replace('\\\\','/')

    entries=dict(pool.map(fetch,entries))

/* ---- 10 minute feed cache ------------------------------------------- */
#define ONET_CACHE_SLOTS 32
#define ONET_CACHE_MS (10ULL * 60 * 1000)
typedef struct { char *url; char *data; size_t size; ULONGLONG at; } onet_cache_entry;
static onet_cache_entry onet_cache[ONET_CACHE_SLOTS];
static ULONGLONG onet_cache_ttl_ms = ONET_CACHE_MS;
static unsigned onet_cache_hits, onet_cache_misses;
static CRITICAL_SECTION onet_cache_lock;
static int onet_cache_ready;
static void onet_cache_init(void) { if (!onet_cache_ready) { InitializeCriticalSection(&onet_cache_lock); onet_cache_ready = 1; } }
static void __attribute__((unused)) onet_cache_clear(void) {
  onet_cache_init();
  EnterCriticalSection(&onet_cache_lock);
  for (int i = 0; i < ONET_CACHE_SLOTS; i++) { free(onet_cache[i].url); free(onet_cache[i].data); memset(&onet_cache[i], 0, sizeof(onet_cache[i])); }
  LeaveCriticalSection(&onet_cache_lock);
}
/* Fetches a small document into memory (<= 16 MiB), serving it from the cache
   when it is younger than the TTL. use_cache == 0 bypasses and refreshes. */
static int onet_fetch(const char *url, const onet_options *base, int use_cache, onet_buffer *out, onet_result *r) {
  onet_options o = *base;
  int slot = -1;
  ULONGLONG now = GetTickCount64();
  onet_cache_init();
  memset(out, 0, sizeof(*out));
  EnterCriticalSection(&onet_cache_lock);
  for (int i = 0; i < ONET_CACHE_SLOTS; i++)
    if (onet_cache[i].url && !strcmp(onet_cache[i].url, url)) {
      if (use_cache && now - onet_cache[i].at < onet_cache_ttl_ms) {

def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("--binary", type=Path, help="optionally compare the current aggregate DLL exports")
    parser.add_argument("--output", type=Path)
    args = parser.parse_args()
    report = check(args.binary)
    output = json.dumps(report, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + "\n"
    if args.output: args.output.write_text(output, encoding="utf-8")
    sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8")
    print(output, end="")
    return int(not report["passed"])


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

#!/usr/bin/env python3
# SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
"""Check compiled EXReader object references against the staged layer graph.

Requires GNU/LLVM nm and an unstripped CMake build. Checks compiled symbols and
the local include graph; semantic type ownership and dead code need review.
"""
import argparse
import json
from pathlib import Path
import re
import shutil
import subprocess


def references(build, nm):
    manifest = build / 'exr-modules.json'
    if manifest.exists():
        configured = json.loads(manifest.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
        objects = []
        for layer in configured['layers']:
            folder = build / f'CMakeFiles/exr_{layer}_objects.dir'
            names = {name + suffix for name, owner in configured['files'].items()
                     if owner == layer for suffix in ('.o', '.obj')}
            objects.extend(p for p in folder.rglob('*') if p.name in names)
        objects.sort()
    else:
        folder = build / 'CMakeFiles/exreader.dir'
        objects = sorted(p for p in folder.rglob('*')
                         if p.name.endswith(('.c.o', '.c.obj')))
    if not objects:
        raise ValueError('No EXReader C objects; build the exreader target first')
    # One invocation avoids spawning a process for each source file.
    result = subprocess.run([nm, '-g', '-P', '-A', *map(str, objects)],
                            check=True, capture_output=True, text=True)
    sources = {p.name.rsplit('.', 1)[0] for p in objects}
    owners, imports = {}, []
    for line in result.stdout.splitlines():
        match = re.match(r'^(.+?): (exr_\w+) ([A-Za-z])(?:\s|$)', line)
        if not match:
            continue
        path, symbol, kind = match.groups()
        source = Path(path).name.rsplit('.', 1)[0]
        if kind == 'U':
            imports.append((source, symbol))
        elif kind.isupper():
            if symbol in owners and owners[symbol] != source:
                raise ValueError(f'Ambiguous symbol {symbol}')
            owners[symbol] = source
    if not owners:
        raise ValueError('nm output contained no EXReader definitions')
    edges = {(source, owners[symbol], symbol) for source, symbol in imports
             if symbol in owners and owners[symbol] != source}
    return sources, edges


def permitted(layers, source, target):
    pending, seen = [source], set()
    while pending:
        layer = pending.pop()
        if layer == target:
            return True
        if layer not in seen:
            seen.add(layer)
            pending.extend(layers[layer])
    return False


def header_references(root, sources, files, layers, headers):
    edges, inverse = set(), set()
    for source in sources:
        origin = root / 'src' / source
        pending, visited = [origin], set()
        while pending:
            path = pending.pop()
            if path in visited:
                continue
            visited.add(path)
            content = re.sub(r'/\*.*?\*/', '', path.read_text(encoding='utf-8'), flags=re.S)
            for include in re.findall(r'^\s*#include\s+"([^"\n]+)"', content, re.M):
                candidates = [path.parent / include, root / 'include' / include]
                target = next((p.resolve() for p in candidates if p.is_file()), None)
                if target is None:
                    continue  # Third-party and build-generated headers are checked by compilation.
                relative = target.relative_to(root).as_posix()
                if relative not in headers:
                    raise ValueError(f'Unclassified local header: {relative}')
                edges.add((source, relative))
                if not permitted(layers, files[source], headers[relative]):
                    inverse.add((source, relative))
                pending.append(target)
    return edges, inverse


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument('build', type=Path)
    parser.add_argument('--nm')
    parser.add_argument('--manifest', type=Path,
                        default=Path(__file__).with_name('layers.json'))
    parser.add_argument('--prune', action='store_true',
                        help='Remove resolved exceptions; never adds exceptions')
    args = parser.parse_args()
    cache = (args.build / 'CMakeCache.txt').read_text(encoding='utf-8')
    cached = re.search(r'^CMAKE_NM:FILEPATH=(.+)$', cache, re.M)
    nm = args.nm or (cached[1] if cached else shutil.which('nm'))
    if not nm:
        parser.error('GNU/LLVM nm is required')
    config = json.loads(args.manifest.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
    layers, files = config['layers'], config['files']
    for layer, deps in layers.items():
        if layer in deps or any(dep not in layers for dep in deps):
            parser.error(f'Invalid layer dependencies: {layer}')
        if any(permitted(layers, dep, layer) for dep in deps):
            parser.error(f'Layer graph contains a cycle: {layer}')

+from pathlib import Path
+from datetime import datetime
+
+root = Path(__file__).parent
+for name in ('build-resume.log', 'layers-resume.log', 'ctest-resume.log'):
+    path = root / name
+    if not path.exists():
+        continue
+    lines = path.read_text(encoding='utf-8', errors='replace').splitlines()
+    if name.startswith('build'):
+        lines = [line for line in lines if line.startswith(('[', 'FAILED', 'BUILD_EXIT=', 'ninja:'))]
+    print(name, '\n' + '\n'.join(lines[-5:]), flush=True)
+print(datetime.now(), flush=True)

diff --git a/C:/Users/Oscar/Documents/Codex/exr-build-cf129/status.py b/C:/Users/Oscar/Documents/Codex/exr-build-cf129/status.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..a6883ba083273ddd0151ed8e976f4a4d4446d2a5
--- /dev/null
+++ b/C:/Users/Oscar/Documents/Codex/exr-build-cf129/status.py
@@ -0,0 +1,20 @@
+from pathlib import Path
+from datetime import datetime
+import re
+
+root = Path(__file__).parent
+for name in ('build-resume.log', 'layers-resume.log', 'ctest-resume.log'):
+    path = root / name
+    if not path.exists():
+        continue
+    lines = path.read_text(encoding='utf-8', errors='replace').splitlines()
+    if name.startswith('ctest'):
+        records = [line for line in lines if re.match(r'^\s*\d+/\d+\s+Test', line)]
+        bad = [line for line in records if any(mark in line for mark in ('***Failed', '***Exception', '***Timeout', 'Not Run'))]
+        print('ctest totals:', 'completed=', len(records), 'passed=', sum('Passed' in line for line in records), 'failed=', len(bad), flush=True)
+        for line in bad:
+            print(line, flush=True)
+    if name.startswith('build'):
+        lines = [line for line in lines if line.startswith(('[', 'FAILED', 'BUILD_EXIT=', 'ninja:'))]
+    print(name, '\n' + '\n'.join(lines[-5:]), flush=True)
+print(datetime.now(), flush=True)
